/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  TOKENS                                                                 */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const INK = "#1C2430";
const STEEL = "#5B6472";
const PAPER = "#F0EFE9";
const PAPER_RAISED = "#FAFAF6";
const AMBER = "#C97A0B";
const RED = "#B0402F";
const GREEN = "#3F7D58";
const LINE = "#D8D5C8";

const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

function expandSeedItems() {
  return SEED_ITEMS.map((r) => ({
    id: uid(), description: r.d, partNumber: r.p, serialNumber: r.s, batchNumber: r.b,
    qty: r.q, unit: r.u, location: r.l, category: r.c, aircraft: r.a,
    status: r.st, expiryDate: r.e, storeType: r.t, remarks: r.r,
  }));
}

function financialYear(dateStr) {
  const d = new Date(dateStr || todayISO());
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const yy = y % 100;
  return d.getMonth() >= 3 ? `${yy}-${yy + 1}` : `${yy - 1}-${yy}`;
}
const todayISO = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const daysUntil = (dateStr) => {
  if (!dateStr) return Infinity;
  const d = new Date(dateStr + "T00:00:00");
  const now = new Date();
  now.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return Math.round((d - now) / 86400000);
};

const UNITS = ["Ea", "Ltr", "Kg", "Gram", "Semkit", "Pinkit", "Quart", "Box", "Set"];
const CATEGORIES_DEFAULT = ["Consumable", "Rotable", "Tool", "Chemical", "Avionics", "Hardware"];
const STORE_TYPES = ["Main", "Bonded", "POL Cupboard", "Fridge"];
const STATUSES = ["Serviceable", "Unserviceable", "ESDS", "New"];

const DEFAULT_DATA = {
  items: [],
  issues: [],
  dispatches: [],
  transfers: [],
  discards: [],
  masters: {
    aircraft: ["VT-RSR", "VT-MUM", "VT-SFU", "VT-POP", "VT-LIB"],
    locations: ["Main Store", "Bonded Store", "POL Cupboard", "Fridge", "Hangar", "Maintenance", "Office"],
    persons: [],
    categories: CATEGORIES_DEFAULT,
    units: [...UNITS, "Ml", "Gal", "Can"],
  },
};

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  STORAGE                                                                */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const STORAGE_KEY = "avss-data-v1";

function useStore() {
  const [data, setData] = useState(DEFAULT_DATA);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await window.storage.get(STORAGE_KEY, false);
        if (res && res.value) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(res.value);
          setData({ ...DEFAULT_DATA, ...parsed, masters: { ...DEFAULT_DATA.masters, ...(parsed.masters || {}) } });
        } else {
          // First ever run: seed with the company's existing stock records
          // (Main / Bonded / POL Cupboard / Fridge) so the store starts populated.
          const seeded = { ...DEFAULT_DATA, items: expandSeedItems() };
          setData(seeded);
          try { await window.storage.set(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(seeded), false); } catch (e2) {}
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // no saved data yet
      }
      setLoaded(true);
    })();
  }, []);

  const persist = useCallback(async (next) => {
    setData(next);
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await window.storage.set(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next), false);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Save failed", e);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }, []);

  return { data, setData: persist, loaded, saving };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  SMALL UI PRIMITIVES                                                    */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

function Placard({ children, style }) {
  return (
    <span
      style={{
        fontFamily: "ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace",
        fontSize: 11,
        letterSpacing: "0.14em",
        textTransform: "uppercase",
        color: STEEL,
        ...style,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

function StatusTag({ status }) {
  const colorMap = {
    Serviceable: GREEN,
    Unserviceable: RED,
    ESDS: AMBER,
    New: INK,
  };
  const c = colorMap[status] || STEEL;
  return (
    <span
      style={{
        display: "inline-flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 5,
        padding: "3px 9px 3px 6px",
        borderRadius: 3,
        border: `1px solid ${c}`,
        color: c,
        fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace",
        fontSize: 10.5,
        fontWeight: 700,
        letterSpacing: "0.06em",
        textTransform: "uppercase",
        whiteSpace: "nowrap",
      }}
    >
      <span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: c, flexShrink: 0 }} />
      {status}
    </span>
  );
}

function ExpiryPill({ dateStr }) {
  if (!dateStr) return <span style={{ color: STEEL, fontSize: 12 }}>—</span>;
  const d = daysUntil(dateStr);
  let color = STEEL, label = dateStr;
  if (d < 0) { color = RED; label = `${dateStr} · EXPIRED`; }
  else if (d <= 10) { color = AMBER; label = `${dateStr} · ${d}d left`; }
  else { color = GREEN; }
  return <span style={{ color, fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>{label}</span>;
}

function Btn({ children, onClick, variant = "primary", style, type = "button", disabled }) {
  const base = {
    fontFamily: "inherit",
    fontSize: 12.5,
    fontWeight: 600,
    letterSpacing: "0.03em",
    padding: "9px 14px",
    borderRadius: 3,
    cursor: disabled ? "default" : "pointer",
    border: "1px solid transparent",
    display: "inline-flex",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 6,
    opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
    transition: "background .12s, border-color .12s",
  };
  const variants = {
    primary: { background: INK, color: PAPER_RAISED, borderColor: INK },
    ghost: { background: "transparent", color: INK, borderColor: LINE },
    danger: { background: "transparent", color: RED, borderColor: RED },
  };
  return (
    <button type={type} disabled={disabled} onClick={onClick} style={{ ...base, ...variants[variant], ...style }}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children, span }) {
  return (
    <label style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 5, gridColumn: span ? `span ${span}` : undefined }}>
      <Placard>{label}</Placard>
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

const inputStyle = {
  fontFamily: "inherit",
  fontSize: 13.5,
  padding: "8px 10px",
  borderRadius: 3,
  border: `1px solid ${LINE}`,
  background: PAPER_RAISED,
  color: INK,
  outline: "none",
};

function Input(props) {
  return <input {...props} style={{ ...inputStyle, ...(props.style || {}) }} />;
}
function Select({ options, allowOther, ...props }) {
  return (
    <select {...props} style={{ ...inputStyle, ...(props.style || {}) }}>
      <option value="">—</option>
      {options.map((o) => <option key={o} value={o}>{o}</option>)}
      {allowOther && <option value="__other__">Other…</option>}
    </select>
  );
}

function Card({ children, style }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ background: PAPER_RAISED, border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, borderRadius: 4, ...style }}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function Empty({ label, action }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: "48px 20px", textAlign: "center", color: STEEL }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 13.5, marginBottom: 10 }}>{label}</div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

function Modal({ title, onClose, children, wide }) {
  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClose}
      style={{
        position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(28,36,48,0.45)",
        display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-start", justifyContent: "center",
        padding: "6vh 16px", zIndex: 50, overflowY: "auto",
      }}
    >
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        style={{
          background: PAPER_RAISED, border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, borderRadius: 6,
          width: "100%", maxWidth: wide ? 760 : 520, boxShadow: "0 12px 40px rgba(28,36,48,0.25)",
        }}
      >
        <div style={{
          display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between",
          padding: "16px 20px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${LINE}`,
        }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 15, letterSpacing: "0.01em", color: INK }}>{title}</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", color: STEEL, padding: 4 }}>
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  NAV                                                                    */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const NAV = [
  { id: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { id: "inventory", label: "Main Inventory", icon: Package },
  { id: "bonded", label: "Bonded Store", icon: Lock },
  { id: "pol", label: "POL / Fridge", icon: FlaskConical },
  { id: "issue", label: "Issue / Voucher", icon: Send },
  { id: "dispatch", label: "Dispatch", icon: Truck },
  { id: "transfer", label: "Stock Transfer", icon: ArrowLeftRight },
  { id: "discard", label: "Discard", icon: Trash2 },
  { id: "audit", label: "Audit History", icon: History },
  { id: "tags", label: "Tag Generator", icon: TagIcon },
  { id: "search", label: "Global Search", icon: Search },
  { id: "import", label: "Bulk Import", icon: UploadCloud },
  { id: "export", label: "Reports & Export", icon: FileSpreadsheet },
  { id: "masters", label: "Settings", icon: Settings },
];

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  APP                                                                     */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
export default function App() {
  const { data, setData, loaded, saving } = useStore();
  const [page, setPage] = useState("dashboard");
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null); // {type, payload}

  const items = data.items;
  const byId = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [items]);

  const lowStockThreshold = 2;
  const expiryWarnDays = 10;

  const flags = useMemo(() => {
    let expired = 0, expiring = 0, low = 0;
    items.forEach((it) => {
      if (it.expiryDate) {
        const d = daysUntil(it.expiryDate);
        if (d < 0) expired++;
        else if (d <= expiryWarnDays) expiring++;
      }
      if (Number(it.qty) <= lowStockThreshold) low++;
    });
    return { expired, expiring, low };
  }, [items]);

  /* ---------- generic mutators ---------- */
  const addAudit = (action, detail) => ({ id: uid(), date: new Date().toISOString(), action, detail });

  const saveItem = (item) => {
    const exists = items.some((i) => i.id === item.id);
    const nextItems = exists ? items.map((i) => (i.id === item.id ? item : i)) : [...items, item];
    setData({ ...data, items: nextItems });
  };
  const deleteItem = (id) => setData({ ...data, items: items.filter((i) => i.id !== id) });

  const addMaster = (key, value) => {
    if (!value) return;
    if (data.masters[key].includes(value)) return;
    setData({ ...data, masters: { ...data.masters, [key]: [...data.masters[key], value] } });
  };

  const doIssue = (rec) => {
    const item = byId[rec.itemId];
    if (!item) return;
    const nextQty = Math.max(0, Number(item.qty) - Number(rec.qty));
    saveNested({
      items: items.map((i) => (i.id === item.id ? { ...i, qty: nextQty } : i)),
      issues: [...data.issues, { ...rec, id: uid(), date: rec.date || todayISO() }],
    });
  };
  const doDispatchBatch = (meta, lines) => {
    const dispatchNo = `ST/IPAPL/${data.dispatches.length + 1}/${financialYear(meta.date)}`;
    const batchId = uid();
    const date = meta.date || todayISO();
    let nextItems = items;
    const newRecs = lines.map((ln) => {
      const item = nextItems.find((i) => i.id === ln.itemId);
      nextItems = nextItems.map((i) => (i.id === ln.itemId ? { ...i, qty: Math.max(0, Number(i.qty) - Number(ln.qty)) } : i));
      return { id: uid(), batchId, dispatchNo, date, itemId: ln.itemId, qty: Number(ln.qty), unit: item?.unit || "", batchNumber: item?.batchNumber || "", ...meta };
    });
    saveNested({ items: nextItems, dispatches: [...data.dispatches, ...newRecs] });
    return { dispatchNo, batchId, records: newRecs };
  };
  const doTransfer = (rec) => {
    const item = byId[rec.itemId];
    if (!item) return;
    saveNested({
      items: items.map((i) => (i.id === item.id ? { ...i, location: rec.to } : i)),
      transfers: [...data.transfers, { ...rec, id: uid(), date: rec.date || todayISO() }],
    });
  };
  const doDiscard = (rec) => {
    const item = byId[rec.itemId];
    if (!item) return;
    const nextQty = Math.max(0, Number(item.qty) - Number(rec.qty));
    saveNested({
      items: items.map((i) => (i.id === item.id ? { ...i, qty: nextQty } : i)),
      discards: [...data.discards, { ...rec, id: uid(), date: rec.date || todayISO() }],
    });
  };
  const saveNested = (patch) => setData({ ...data, ...patch });

  if (!loaded) {
    return (
      <div style={{ height: "100vh", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", background: PAPER, color: STEEL, fontFamily: "system-ui" }}>
        Loading store data…
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", height: "100vh", background: PAPER, fontFamily: "system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif", color: INK }}>
      {/* SIDEBAR */}
      <div style={{ width: 224, background: INK, color: PAPER_RAISED, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", flexShrink: 0 }}>
        <div style={{ padding: "20px 18px 16px", borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: "0.18em", opacity: 0.55, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>MRO · STORES</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 16.5, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: "0.01em", marginTop: 3 }}>Aviation Store</div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: "auto", padding: "10px 8px" }}>
          {NAV.map((n) => {
            const Icon = n.icon;
            const active = page === n.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={n.id}
                onClick={() => setPage(n.id)}
                style={{
                  width: "100%", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 9,
                  padding: "9px 10px", marginBottom: 2, borderRadius: 3, border: "none",
                  cursor: "pointer", textAlign: "left", fontSize: 12.8, fontWeight: active ? 700 : 500,
                  background: active ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.11)" : "transparent",
                  color: active ? PAPER_RAISED : "rgba(250,250,246,0.72)",
                }}
              >
                <Icon size={15} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                {n.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div style={{ padding: "12px 18px", borderTop: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)", fontSize: 10.5, color: "rgba(250,250,246,0.4)", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>
          {saving ? "SAVING…" : "SAVED LOCALLY"}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MAIN */}
      <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: "auto" }}>
        <div style={{ maxWidth: 1180, margin: "0 auto", padding: "28px 32px 60px" }}>
          {page === "dashboard" && <Dashboard data={data} />}
          {page === "inventory" && <InventoryPage title="Main Inventory" storeType="Main" data={data} onSave={saveItem} onDelete={deleteItem} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "bonded" && <InventoryPage title="Bonded Store" storeType="Bonded" data={data} onSave={saveItem} onDelete={deleteItem} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "pol" && <PolPage data={data} onSave={saveItem} onDelete={deleteItem} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "issue" && <IssuePage data={data} onIssue={doIssue} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "dispatch" && <DispatchPage data={data} onDispatchBatch={doDispatchBatch} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "transfer" && <TransferPage data={data} onTransfer={doTransfer} addMaster={addMaster} />}
          {page === "discard" && <DiscardPage data={data} onDiscard={doDiscard} />}
          {page === "lowstock" && <LowStockPage data={data} threshold={lowStockThreshold} warnDays={expiryWarnDays} />}
          {page === "audit" && <AuditPage data={data} byId={byId} />}
          {page === "tags" && <TagsPage data={data} />}
          {page === "search" && <SearchPage data={data} />}
          {page === "import" && <ImportPage data={data} setData={setData} />}
          {page === "export" && <ExportPage data={data} />}
          {page === "masters" && <MastersPage data={data} setData={setData} flags={flags} setPage={setPage} thresholds={{ lowStockThreshold, expiryWarnDays }} />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  DASHBOARD                                                              */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function PageHeader({ eyebrow, title, action }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-end", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 22, borderBottom: `2px solid ${INK}`, paddingBottom: 14 }}>
      <div>
        <Placard>{eyebrow}</Placard>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800, marginTop: 2, letterSpacing: "-0.01em" }}>{title}</div>
      </div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

function StatBlock({ label, value, color }) {
  return (
    <Card style={{ padding: "16px 18px", flex: 1, minWidth: 140 }}>
      <Placard>{label}</Placard>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, marginTop: 6, color: color || INK, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>{value}</div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function Dashboard({ data }) {
  const recent = [
    ...data.issues.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Issue" })),
    ...data.dispatches.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Dispatch" })),
    ...data.transfers.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Transfer" })),
    ...data.discards.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Discard" })),
  ].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date)).slice(0, 8);

  const byId = Object.fromEntries(data.items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
  const byStore = (t) => data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === t).length;

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Overview" title="Dashboard" />
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <StatBlock label="Total Line Items" value={data.items.length} />
        <StatBlock label="Main Inventory" value={byStore("Main")} />
        <StatBlock label="Bonded Store" value={byStore("Bonded")} />
        <StatBlock label="POL / Fridge" value={byStore("POL Cupboard") + byStore("Fridge")} />
        <StatBlock label="Serviceable" value={data.items.filter((i) => i.status === "Serviceable").length} color={GREEN} />
      </div>

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 10 }}>Recent Activity</Placard>
      <Card>
        {recent.length === 0 ? (
          <Empty label="No movement recorded yet. Issue, dispatch, or transfer an item to see activity here." />
        ) : (
          <Table
            columns={["Type", "Item", "Qty", "Detail", "Date"]}
            rows={recent.map((r) => [
              r.type,
              byId[r.itemId]?.description || "—",
              `${r.qty} ${byId[r.itemId]?.unit || ""}`,
              r.issuedTo || r.dispatchTo || r.to || r.reason || "—",
              r.date?.slice(0, 10),
            ])}
          />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function Table({ columns, rows, onRowClick }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
      <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12.8 }}>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            {columns.map((c) => (
              <th key={c} style={{ textAlign: "left", padding: "10px 14px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${LINE}`, color: STEEL, fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: "0.08em", textTransform: "uppercase", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontWeight: 600 }}>
                {c}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r, i) => (
            <tr key={i} onClick={() => onRowClick && onRowClick(i)} style={{ cursor: onRowClick ? "pointer" : "default" }}>
              {r.map((cell, j) => (
                <td key={j} style={{ padding: "10px 14px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${LINE}` }}>{cell}</td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  ITEM FORM (shared by Inventory / Bonded / POL)                         */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function ItemForm({ initial, storeTypeFixed, onSave, onClose, masters, addMaster }) {
  const [f, setF] = useState(initial || {
    id: uid(), description: "", partNumber: "", serialNumber: "", batchNumber: "",
    qty: "", unit: "Ea", location: "", category: "", aircraft: "", status: "New",
    expiryDate: "", storeType: storeTypeFixed || "Main", remarks: "",
  });
  const set = (k) => (e) => {
    let v = e && e.target ? e.target.value : e;
    setF((prev) => ({ ...prev, [k]: v }));
  };
  const handleOther = (k, masterKey) => (e) => {
    const v = e.target.value;
    if (v === "__other__") {
      const custom = prompt(`Enter new ${masterKey}:`);
      if (custom) { addMaster(masterKey, custom); setF((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: custom })); }
    } else {
      setF((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }));
    }
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!f.description) return; onSave(f); onClose(); }}>
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14 }}>
        <Field label="Description" span={2}><Input required value={f.description} onChange={set("description")} placeholder="Item name" /></Field>
        <Field label="Part Number"><Input value={f.partNumber} onChange={set("partNumber")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Serial Number"><Input value={f.serialNumber} onChange={set("serialNumber")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Batch / Lot Number"><Input value={f.batchNumber} onChange={set("batchNumber")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Category">
          <Select options={masters.categories} allowOther value={f.category} onChange={handleOther("category", "categories")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Quantity"><Input type="number" min="0" value={f.qty} onChange={set("qty")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Unit"><Select options={masters.units} value={f.unit} onChange={set("unit")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Location">
          <Select options={masters.locations} allowOther value={f.location} onChange={handleOther("location", "locations")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Aircraft">
          <Select options={masters.aircraft} allowOther value={f.aircraft} onChange={handleOther("aircraft", "aircraft")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Status"><Select options={STATUSES} value={f.status} onChange={set("status")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Store Type">
          <Select options={STORE_TYPES} value={f.storeType} onChange={set("storeType")} disabled={!!storeTypeFixed} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Expiry Date"><Input type="date" value={f.expiryDate} onChange={set("expiryDate")} /></Field>
        <Field label="Remarks" span={2}><Input value={f.remarks} onChange={set("remarks")} /></Field>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 8, marginTop: 20 }}>
        <Btn variant="ghost" onClick={onClose} type="button">Cancel</Btn>
        <Btn type="submit">Save Item</Btn>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  INVENTORY PAGE (Main / Bonded)                                         */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function InventoryPage({ title, storeType, data, onSave, onDelete, addMaster }) {
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [cat, setCat] = useState("");
  const [ac, setAc] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  const rows = data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === storeType).filter((i) => {
    if (q && !`${i.description} ${i.partNumber} ${i.serialNumber} ${i.batchNumber}`.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())) return false;
    if (cat && i.category !== cat) return false;
    if (ac && i.aircraft !== ac) return false;
    if (status && i.status !== status) return false;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow={`Store · ${storeType}`} title={title}
        action={<Btn onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "new" })}><Plus size={14} /> New Item</Btn>} />

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginBottom: 16, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <Input placeholder="Search description, part no, serial, batch…" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 220 }} />
        <Select options={data.masters.categories} value={cat} onChange={(e) => setCat(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 150 }} />
        <Select options={data.masters.aircraft} value={ac} onChange={(e) => setAc(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 130 }} />
        <Select options={STATUSES} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 150 }} />
      </div>

      <Card>
        {rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Empty label={`No items in ${storeType} store match your filters.`} action={<Btn onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "new" })}>Add the first item</Btn>} />
        ) : (
          <Table
            columns={["Description", "Part No", "Batch/Serial", "Qty", "Location", "Aircraft", "Status", "Expiry", ""]}
            rows={rows.map((it) => [
              it.description,
              <span style={{ fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>{it.partNumber || "—"}</span>,
              <span style={{ fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontSize: 11.5 }}>{[it.batchNumber, it.serialNumber].filter(Boolean).join(" / ") || "—"}</span>,
              `${it.qty} ${it.unit}`,
              it.location || "—",
              it.aircraft || "—",
              <StatusTag status={it.status} />,
              <ExpiryPill dateStr={it.expiryDate} />,
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6 }}>
                <button onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "edit", item: it })} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: STEEL, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12 }}>Edit</button>
                <button onClick={() => { if (confirm("Delete this item?")) onDelete(it.id); }} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: RED, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12 }}>Delete</button>
              </div>,
            ])}
          />
        )}
      </Card>

      {modal && (
        <Modal title={modal.mode === "new" ? "New Item" : "Edit Item"} onClose={() => setModal(null)}>
          <ItemForm initial={modal.item} storeTypeFixed={storeType} onSave={onSave} onClose={() => setModal(null)} masters={data.masters} addMaster={addMaster} />
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function PolPage({ data, onSave, onDelete, addMaster }) {
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
  const rows = data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "POL Cupboard" || i.storeType === "Fridge");
  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Temperature & Expiry Controlled" title="POL Cupboard / Fridge"
        action={<Btn onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "new" })}><Plus size={14} /> New Item</Btn>} />
      <Card>
        {rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Empty label="No oils, sealants, or chemicals recorded yet." action={<Btn onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "new" })}>Add the first item</Btn>} />
        ) : (
          <Table
            columns={["Description", "Batch", "Qty", "Store Type", "Expiry", "Status", ""]}
            rows={rows.map((it) => [
              it.description,
              <span style={{ fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>{it.batchNumber || "—"}</span>,
              `${it.qty} ${it.unit}`,
              it.storeType,
              <ExpiryPill dateStr={it.expiryDate} />,
              <StatusTag status={it.status} />,
              <button onClick={() => setModal({ mode: "edit", item: it })} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: STEEL, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12 }}>Edit</button>,
            ])}
          />
        )}
      </Card>
      {modal && (
        <Modal title={modal.mode === "new" ? "New POL / Fridge Item" : "Edit Item"} onClose={() => setModal(null)}>
          <ItemForm initial={modal.item || { storeType: "POL Cupboard" }} onSave={onSave} onClose={() => setModal(null)} masters={data.masters} addMaster={addMaster} />
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  MOVEMENT FORM helper                                                   */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function ItemPicker({ items, value, onChange }) {
  const item = items.find((i) => i.id === value);
  return (
    <Field label="Item">
      <Select value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        options={[]}
      >
        <option value="">Select item…</option>
        {items.map((i) => (
          <option key={i.id} value={i.id}>{i.description} — {i.qty} {i.unit} avail.{i.partNumber ? ` (${i.partNumber})` : ""}</option>
        ))}
      </Select>
      {item && Number(item.qty) <= 0 && <div style={{ color: RED, fontSize: 11.5, marginTop: 3 }}>No stock available for this item.</div>}
    </Field>
  );
}
// Select needs children support for ItemPicker — patch below by redefining inline usage.

function IssuePage({ data, onIssue, addMaster }) {
  const [itemId, setItemId] = useState("");
  const [qty, setQty] = useState("");
  const [issuedTo, setIssuedTo] = useState("");
  const [aircraft, setAircraft] = useState("");
  const [location, setLocation] = useState("");
  const [purpose, setPurpose] = useState("");
  const item = data.items.find((i) => i.id === itemId);

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!item || !qty) return;
    if (Number(qty) > Number(item.qty)) { alert("Issue quantity exceeds available stock."); return; }
    onIssue({ itemId, qty: Number(qty), unit: item.unit, issuedTo: issuedTo.toUpperCase(), aircraft, location, purpose });
    if (issuedTo) addMaster("persons", issuedTo.toUpperCase());
    setItemId(""); setQty(""); setIssuedTo(""); setAircraft(""); setLocation(""); setPurpose("");
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Voucher" title="Issue Item" />
      <Card style={{ padding: 20, maxWidth: 640 }}>
        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ gridColumn: "span 2" }}>
              <Placard>Item</Placard>
              <select value={itemId} onChange={(e) => setItemId(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: "100%", marginTop: 5 }}>
                <option value="">Select item…</option>
                {data.items.filter((i) => Number(i.qty) > 0).map((i) => (
                  <option key={i.id} value={i.id}>{i.description} — {i.qty} {i.unit} available</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <Field label="Issue Qty"><Input type="number" min="1" max={item?.qty || undefined} value={qty} onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Issued To"><Input value={issuedTo} onChange={(e) => setIssuedTo(e.target.value)} placeholder="Technician / engineer" /></Field>
            <Field label="Aircraft"><Select options={data.masters.aircraft} value={aircraft} onChange={(e) => setAircraft(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Issue Location"><Select options={data.masters.locations} value={location} onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Purpose / Remarks" span={2}><Input value={purpose} onChange={(e) => setPurpose(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 18 }}>
            <Btn type="submit" disabled={!item || !qty}>Record Issue</Btn>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", margin: "26px 0 10px" }}>Issue Register</Placard>
      <Card>
        {data.issues.length === 0 ? <Empty label="No issues recorded yet." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Item", "Qty", "Issued To", "Aircraft", "Location", "Date"]}
            rows={[...data.issues].reverse().map((r) => {
              const it = data.items.find((i) => i.id === r.itemId);
              return [it?.description || "—", `${r.qty} ${r.unit}`, r.issuedTo || "—", r.aircraft || "—", r.location || "—", r.date?.slice(0, 10)];
            })} />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function DispatchNotePrintable({ meta, lines, byId, onClose }) {
  return (
    <Modal title={`Dispatch Note · ${meta.dispatchNo}`} onClose={onClose} wide>
      <div className="dispatch-print" style={{ background: "#fff", padding: 0 }}>
        <style>{`
          @media print {
            body * { visibility: hidden; }
            .dispatch-print, .dispatch-print * { visibility: visible; }
            .dispatch-print { position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; }
            .no-print { display: none !important; }
          }
        `}</style>
        <div style={{ border: `2px solid ${INK}`, padding: 24, fontFamily: "Georgia, 'Times New Roman', serif", color: "#111" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", borderBottom: `2.5px solid ${INK}`, paddingBottom: 14, marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ width: 42, height: 42, borderRadius: "50%", border: `2px solid ${INK}`, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 15 }}>✈</div>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 19, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: "0.01em" }}>Indopacific Aviation Pvt Ltd</div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: "0.1em", color: STEEL, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>Store Management Record</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{ textAlign: "right", fontSize: 11.5, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>
              <div>DATE: <b>{meta.date}</b></div>
              <div>DISPATCH NO: <b>{meta.dispatchNo}</b></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: "0.12em", marginBottom: 16 }}>DISPATCH NOTE</div>

          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14, marginBottom: 16, fontSize: 12.5 }}>
            <div style={{ border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, padding: 10 }}>
              <Placard style={{ color: STEEL }}>From</Placard>
              <div style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>{meta.fromLocation || "—"}{meta.dispatchBy ? ` · ${meta.dispatchBy}` : ""}</div>
            </div>
            <div style={{ border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, padding: 10 }}>
              <Placard style={{ color: STEEL }}>To</Placard>
              <div style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>{meta.destination || "—"}{meta.dispatchTo ? ` · ${meta.dispatchTo}` : ""}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ background: "#EFF3F0", textAlign: "center", padding: "6px 0", fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: "0.05em", marginBottom: 2 }}>
            STOCK TRANSFER{meta.aircraft ? ` — FOR ${meta.aircraft}` : ""}
          </div>

          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 12 }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: "#1C2430", color: "#fff" }}>
                {["Sr.", "Description", "P/N", "Qty", "Batch No"].map((h) => (
                  <th key={h} style={{ padding: "7px 8px", textAlign: "left", fontSize: 10.5, letterSpacing: "0.06em", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>{h}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {lines.map((ln, i) => {
                const it = byId[ln.itemId];
                return (
                  <tr key={i} style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${LINE}` }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: "6px 8px" }}>{String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "6px 8px" }}>{it?.description || "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "6px 8px", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace" }}>{it?.partNumber || "—"}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "6px 8px" }}>{ln.qty} {ln.unit}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "6px 8px" }}>{it?.batchNumber || "-"}</td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>

          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 44 }}>
            <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
              <div style={{ borderTop: `1px solid ${INK}`, width: 180, paddingTop: 4, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: "0.05em" }}>AUTHORISED SIGNATORY</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ background: "#F0EFE9", textAlign: "center", padding: "6px 0", fontSize: 10, color: STEEL, marginTop: 18 }}>
            This is a computer-generated document, no signature required for internal transfer
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="no-print" style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Btn variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Close</Btn>
        <Btn onClick={() => window.print()}><Printer size={14} /> Print</Btn>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function DispatchPage({ data, onDispatchBatch, addMaster }) {
  const [lineItemId, setLineItemId] = useState("");
  const [lineQty, setLineQty] = useState("");
  const [lines, setLines] = useState([]); // {itemId, qty, unit}
  const [dispatchTo, setDispatchTo] = useState("");
  const [dispatchBy, setDispatchBy] = useState("");
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState("");
  const [fromLocation, setFromLocation] = useState("");
  const [aircraft, setAircraft] = useState("");
  const [printView, setPrintView] = useState(null);

  const byId = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(data.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [data.items]);
  const availableForLine = (id) => {
    const already = lines.filter((l) => l.itemId === id).reduce((s, l) => s + Number(l.qty), 0);
    const item = byId[id];
    return item ? Number(item.qty) - already : 0;
  };

  const addLine = () => {
    const item = byId[lineItemId];
    if (!item || !lineQty) return;
    if (Number(lineQty) > availableForLine(lineItemId)) { alert("Quantity exceeds available stock."); return; }
    setLines((prev) => [...prev, { itemId: lineItemId, qty: Number(lineQty), unit: item.unit }]);
    setLineItemId(""); setLineQty("");
  };
  const removeLine = (idx) => setLines((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (lines.length === 0 || !destination) return;
    const meta = { destination, dispatchTo: dispatchTo.toUpperCase(), dispatchBy: dispatchBy.toUpperCase(), fromLocation, aircraft, date: todayISO() };
    const { dispatchNo, records } = onDispatchBatch(meta, lines);
    setPrintView({ meta: { ...meta, dispatchNo }, lines: records });
    setLines([]); setDispatchTo(""); setDispatchBy(""); setDestination(""); setFromLocation(""); setAircraft("");
  };

  // group register by dispatchNo
  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map();
    [...data.dispatches].reverse().forEach((r) => {
      const key = r.dispatchNo || r.id;
      if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, []);
      map.get(key).push(r);
    });
    return [...map.entries()];
  }, [data.dispatches]);

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Print-ready" title="Dispatch Note" />
      <Card style={{ padding: 20, maxWidth: 720 }}>
        <Placard style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 8 }}>Add Line Items</Placard>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginBottom: 10, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <select value={lineItemId} onChange={(e) => setLineItemId(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2, minWidth: 220 }}>
            <option value="">Select item…</option>
            {data.items.filter((i) => Number(i.qty) > 0).map((i) => (
              <option key={i.id} value={i.id}>{i.description} — {i.qty} {i.unit} available{i.partNumber ? ` (${i.partNumber})` : ""}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <Input type="number" min="1" placeholder="Qty" value={lineQty} onChange={(e) => setLineQty(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 90 }} />
          <Btn variant="ghost" type="button" onClick={addLine}><Plus size={14} /> Add</Btn>
        </div>

        {lines.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <Table columns={["Item", "Qty", ""]} rows={lines.map((l, i) => [
              byId[l.itemId]?.description || "—", `${l.qty} ${l.unit}`,
              <button onClick={() => removeLine(i)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: RED, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12 }}>Remove</button>,
            ])} />
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14 }}>
            <Field label="Destination"><Input value={destination} onChange={(e) => setDestination(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Indopacific Aviation MRO, Hyderabad" /></Field>
            <Field label="Dispatch To"><Input value={dispatchTo} onChange={(e) => setDispatchTo(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Dispatch By"><Input value={dispatchBy} onChange={(e) => setDispatchBy(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="From Location"><Select options={data.masters.locations} value={fromLocation} onChange={(e) => setFromLocation(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Aircraft (optional)"><Select options={data.masters.aircraft} value={aircraft} onChange={(e) => setAircraft(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 18 }}>
            <Btn type="submit" disabled={lines.length === 0 || !destination}><Printer size={14} /> Create & Print Dispatch Note</Btn>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", margin: "26px 0 10px" }}>Dispatch Register</Placard>
      <Card>
        {grouped.length === 0 ? <Empty label="No dispatches recorded yet." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Dispatch No", "Items", "Destination", "Dispatch To", "Date", ""]}
            rows={grouped.map(([key, recs]) => [
              <span style={{ fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontSize: 11.5 }}>{recs[0].dispatchNo || "—"}</span>,
              `${recs.length} item${recs.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}`,
              recs[0].destination || "—",
              recs[0].dispatchTo || "—",
              recs[0].date?.slice(0, 10),
              <button onClick={() => setPrintView({ meta: recs[0], lines: recs })} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", color: STEEL, cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 12, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 4 }}><Printer size={12} /> Print</button>,
            ])} />
        )}
      </Card>

      {printView && <DispatchNotePrintable meta={printView.meta} lines={printView.lines} byId={byId} onClose={() => setPrintView(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function TransferPage({ data, onTransfer, addMaster }) {
  const [itemId, setItemId] = useState("");
  const [qty, setQty] = useState("");
  const [from, setFrom] = useState("");
  const [to, setTo] = useState("");
  const item = data.items.find((i) => i.id === itemId);

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!item || !to) return;
    onTransfer({ itemId, qty: qty || item.qty, unit: item.unit, from: from || item.location, to });
    setItemId(""); setQty(""); setFrom(""); setTo("");
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Movement" title="Stock Transfer" />
      <Card style={{ padding: 20, maxWidth: 640 }}>
        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ gridColumn: "span 2" }}>
              <Placard>Item</Placard>
              <select value={itemId} onChange={(e) => { setItemId(e.target.value); const it = data.items.find(i => i.id === e.target.value); setFrom(it?.location || ""); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: "100%", marginTop: 5 }}>
                <option value="">Select item…</option>
                {data.items.map((i) => (
                  <option key={i.id} value={i.id}>{i.description} — currently at {i.location || "unspecified"}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <Field label="Qty (blank = full)"><Input type="number" min="1" value={qty} onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)} placeholder={item ? String(item.qty) : ""} /></Field>
            <Field label="From"><Input value={from} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="To" span={2}><Select options={data.masters.locations} value={to} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 18 }}>
            <Btn type="submit" disabled={!item || !to}>Record Transfer</Btn>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", margin: "26px 0 10px" }}>Transfer History</Placard>
      <Card>
        {data.transfers.length === 0 ? <Empty label="No transfers recorded yet." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Item", "Qty", "From", "To", "Date"]}
            rows={[...data.transfers].reverse().map((r) => {
              const it = data.items.find((i) => i.id === r.itemId);
              return [it?.description || "—", `${r.qty} ${r.unit || ""}`, r.from || "—", r.to || "—", r.date?.slice(0, 10)];
            })} />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function DiscardPage({ data, onDiscard }) {
  const [itemId, setItemId] = useState("");
  const [qty, setQty] = useState("");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("Expired");
  const item = data.items.find((i) => i.id === itemId);

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!item || !qty) return;
    if (Number(qty) > Number(item.qty)) { alert("Discard quantity exceeds available stock."); return; }
    onDiscard({ itemId, qty: Number(qty), unit: item.unit, reason });
    setItemId(""); setQty("");
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Removal" title="Discard / Scrap" />
      <Card style={{ padding: 20, maxWidth: 560 }}>
        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ gridColumn: "span 2" }}>
              <Placard>Item</Placard>
              <select value={itemId} onChange={(e) => setItemId(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: "100%", marginTop: 5 }}>
                <option value="">Select item…</option>
                {data.items.filter((i) => Number(i.qty) > 0).map((i) => (
                  <option key={i.id} value={i.id}>{i.description} — {i.qty} {i.unit} available</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <Field label="Discard Qty"><Input type="number" min="1" max={item?.qty || undefined} value={qty} onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <Field label="Reason"><Select options={["Expired", "Damaged", "Beyond repair"]} value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 18 }}>
            <Btn variant="danger" type="submit" disabled={!item || !qty}>Confirm Discard</Btn>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", margin: "26px 0 10px" }}>Discard Register</Placard>
      <Card>
        {data.discards.length === 0 ? <Empty label="No discards recorded yet." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Item", "Qty", "Reason", "Date"]}
            rows={[...data.discards].reverse().map((r) => {
              const it = data.items.find((i) => i.id === r.itemId);
              return [it?.description || "—", `${r.qty} ${r.unit}`, r.reason, r.date?.slice(0, 10)];
            })} />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  LOW STOCK & EXPIRY                                                     */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function LowStockPage({ data, threshold, warnDays }) {
  const expired = data.items.filter((i) => i.expiryDate && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) < 0);
  const expiring = data.items.filter((i) => i.expiryDate && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) >= 0 && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) <= warnDays);
  const low = data.items.filter((i) => Number(i.qty) <= threshold);

  const Section = ({ label, color, rows }) => (
    <div style={{ marginBottom: 26 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, marginBottom: 10 }}>
        <span style={{ width: 9, height: 9, borderRadius: "50%", background: color }} />
        <Placard style={{ color: INK, fontSize: 12 }}>{label} ({rows.length})</Placard>
      </div>
      <Card>
        {rows.length === 0 ? <Empty label="None." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Description", "Part No", "Qty", "Location", "Expiry", "Status"]}
            rows={rows.map((it) => [it.description, it.partNumber || "—", `${it.qty} ${it.unit}`, it.location || "—", <ExpiryPill dateStr={it.expiryDate} />, <StatusTag status={it.status} />])} />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Colour-coded" title="Low Stock & Expiry" />
      <Section label="Expired" color={RED} rows={expired} />
      <Section label={`Expiring within ${warnDays} days`} color={AMBER} rows={expiring} />
      <Section label={`Low quantity (≤ ${threshold})`} color={AMBER} rows={low} />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  AUDIT                                                                   */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function AuditPage({ data, byId }) {
  const all = [
    ...data.issues.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Issue", who: r.issuedTo, where: r.location })),
    ...data.dispatches.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Dispatch", who: r.dispatchTo, where: r.destination })),
    ...data.transfers.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Transfer", who: "—", where: `${r.from} → ${r.to}` })),
    ...data.discards.map((r) => ({ ...r, type: "Discard", who: "—", where: r.reason })),
  ].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Full History" title="Audit History" />
      <Card>
        {all.length === 0 ? <Empty label="No movement recorded yet." /> : (
          <Table columns={["Type", "Item", "Qty", "Who / Reason", "Where", "Date"]}
            rows={all.map((r) => [r.type, byId[r.itemId]?.description || "—", `${r.qty} ${r.unit || ""}`, r.who || "—", r.where || "—", r.date?.slice(0, 10)])} />
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  TAGS                                                                    */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function TagCard({ item, size }) {
  const w = size === "Large" ? 300 : 240;
  return (
    <div style={{
      width: w, background: PAPER_RAISED, border: `1.5px solid ${INK}`, borderRadius: 4,
      padding: "14px 16px 12px", position: "relative", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace",
      breakInside: "avoid",
    }}>
      <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: 10, left: -7, width: 14, height: 14, borderRadius: "50%", background: PAPER, border: `1.5px solid ${INK}` }} />
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", marginBottom: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 9.5, letterSpacing: "0.14em", color: STEEL }}>AVIATION STORES</div>
        <StatusTag status={item.status} />
      </div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8, lineHeight: 1.3 }}>{item.description}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 10.5, lineHeight: 1.9, color: INK }}>
        <div>P/N: <b>{item.partNumber || "—"}</b></div>
        <div>S/N: <b>{item.serialNumber || "—"}</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;BATCH: <b>{item.batchNumber || "—"}</b></div>
        <div>QTY: <b>{item.qty} {item.unit}</b>&nbsp;&nbsp;LOC: <b>{item.location || "—"}</b></div>
        {item.expiryDate && <div>EXP: <b>{item.expiryDate}</b></div>}
      </div>
      <div style={{ marginTop: 10, paddingTop: 8, borderTop: `1px dashed ${LINE}`, fontSize: 9, color: STEEL, display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
        <span>DATE: {todayISO()}</span>
        <span>SIGN: __________</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function TagsPage({ data }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const [size, setSize] = useState("Medium");
  const toggle = (id) => setSelected((s) => (s.includes(id) ? s.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...s, id]));

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Print-ready" title="Tag Generator"
        action={selected.length > 0 && <Btn onClick={() => window.print()}><Printer size={14} /> Print {selected.length} Tag{selected.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}</Btn>} />

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 20 }}>
        <Card style={{ flex: 1, maxWidth: 380, padding: 16 }}>
          <Placard style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 10 }}>Select Items</Placard>
          <Field label="Tag Size"><Select options={["Medium", "Large"]} value={size} onChange={(e) => setSize(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 14, maxHeight: 420, overflowY: "auto", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 6 }}>
            {data.items.length === 0 && <Empty label="No items yet — add inventory first." />}
            {data.items.map((it) => (
              <label key={it.id} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8, padding: "6px 8px", borderRadius: 3, border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, fontSize: 12.5, cursor: "pointer" }}>
                <input type="checkbox" checked={selected.includes(it.id)} onChange={() => toggle(it.id)} />
                {it.description} <span style={{ color: STEEL, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontSize: 11 }}>({it.partNumber || "no P/N"})</span>
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>

        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 14, alignContent: "flex-start" }}>
          {selected.length === 0 ? (
            <Empty label="Select items on the left to preview their tags here." />
          ) : (
            data.items.filter((i) => selected.includes(i.id)).map((it) => <TagCard key={it.id} item={it} size={size} />)
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  GLOBAL SEARCH                                                          */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function SearchPage({ data }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const results = q.length < 1 ? [] : data.items.filter((i) =>
    `${i.description} ${i.partNumber} ${i.serialNumber} ${i.batchNumber}`.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())
  );

  const movementsFor = (id) => ({
    issues: data.issues.filter((r) => r.itemId === id),
    dispatches: data.dispatches.filter((r) => r.itemId === id),
    transfers: data.transfers.filter((r) => r.itemId === id),
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Part / Serial / Batch" title="Global Search" />
      <Input autoFocus placeholder="Search by description, part number, serial, or batch…" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} style={{ width: "100%", marginBottom: 18, padding: "12px 14px", fontSize: 15 }} />
      {q.length >= 1 && results.length === 0 && <Empty label="No matching items found." />}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 14 }}>
        {results.map((it) => {
          const mv = movementsFor(it.id);
          const total = mv.issues.length + mv.dispatches.length + mv.transfers.length;
          return (
            <Card key={it.id} style={{ padding: 16 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14.5 }}>{it.description}</div>
                  <Placard style={{ marginTop: 3 }}>{it.partNumber || "no P/N"} · {it.storeType} · {it.location || "no location"}</Placard>
                </div>
                <StatusTag status={it.status} />
              </div>
              <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12.5, color: STEEL }}>
                {it.qty} {it.unit} available · {total} movement record{total !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
                {it.expiryDate && <> · expiry <ExpiryPill dateStr={it.expiryDate} /></>}
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  BULK IMPORT                                                             */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const HEADER_HINTS = {
  description: ["description", "desc", "item name", "item"],
  partNumber: ["part no", "part number", "p/n", "pn", "partno"],
  serialNumber: ["serial no", "serial number", "s/n", "sn"],
  batchNumber: ["batch no", "batch number", "lot no", "lot"],
  qty: ["qty", "quantity", "balance", "rcvd qty"],
  unit: ["unit"],
  aircraft: ["aircraft", "a/c", "ac"],
  location: ["location", "loc", "rack"],
  status: ["condition", "status"],
  expiryDate: ["expiry", "exp.date", "exp date", "outgoing/expiry"],
  remarks: ["remarks", "remark", "notes"],
  storeType: ["store", "source", "store type"],
};

function normHeader(s) { return String(s || "").trim().toLowerCase(); }

function detectHeaderRow(rows) {
  let best = 0, bestScore = -1;
  for (let r = 0; r < Math.min(rows.length, 6); r++) {
    const cells = (rows[r] || []).map(normHeader);
    let score = 0;
    Object.values(HEADER_HINTS).forEach((hints) => {
      if (cells.some((c) => hints.some((h) => c.includes(h)))) score++;
    });
    if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = r; }
  }
  return bestScore >= 2 ? best : -1;
}

function buildColumnMap(headerRow) {
  const cells = headerRow.map(normHeader);
  const map = {};
  Object.entries(HEADER_HINTS).forEach(([field, hints]) => {
    let idx = cells.findIndex((c) => hints.some((h) => c === h));
    if (idx === -1) idx = cells.findIndex((c) => hints.some((h) => c.includes(h)));
    if (idx !== -1) map[field] = idx;
  });
  return map;
}

function detectStoreType(text) {
  const s = String(text || "").toUpperCase();
  if (s.includes("BONDED")) return "Bonded";
  if (s.includes("FRIDGE")) return "Fridge";
  if (s.includes("POL")) return "POL Cupboard";
  if (s.includes("MAIN")) return "Main";
  return "";
}

function parseRowsToItems(rows, defaultStoreType) {
  const headerIdx = detectHeaderRow(rows);
  if (headerIdx === -1) return { error: "Could not find a recognisable header row (needs at least Description, Part No, or Qty)." };
  const colMap = buildColumnMap(rows[headerIdx]);
  if (colMap.description === undefined && colMap.partNumber === undefined) {
    return { error: "Could not find a Description or Part Number column." };
  }
  const items = [];
  for (let r = headerIdx + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const row = rows[r] || [];
    const get = (f) => (colMap[f] !== undefined ? row[colMap[f]] : undefined);
    const description = get("description") != null ? String(get("description")).trim() : "";
    const partNumber = get("partNumber") != null ? String(get("partNumber")).trim() : "";
    if (!description && !partNumber) continue;
    let qtyRaw = get("qty");
    let qty = 0;
    if (qtyRaw != null) {
      const m = String(qtyRaw).match(/[\d.]+/);
      qty = m ? parseFloat(m[0]) : 0;
    }
    const statusRaw = get("status") != null ? String(get("status")).trim().toUpperCase() : "";
    const status = statusRaw === "SERVICEABLE" ? "Serviceable" : statusRaw === "UNSERVICEABLE" ? "Unserviceable" : statusRaw === "ESDS" ? "ESDS" : "New";
    const expiryRaw = get("expiryDate") != null ? String(get("expiryDate")).trim() : "";
    const expiryDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(expiryRaw) ? expiryRaw : "";
    const storeHint = get("storeType") != null ? String(get("storeType")) : (get("location") != null ? String(get("location")) : "");
    items.push({
      id: uid(),
      description: description || partNumber,
      partNumber,
      serialNumber: get("serialNumber") != null ? String(get("serialNumber")).trim() : "",
      batchNumber: get("batchNumber") != null ? String(get("batchNumber")).trim() : "",
      qty,
      unit: get("unit") != null ? String(get("unit")).trim() || "Ea" : "Ea",
      location: get("location") != null ? String(get("location")).trim() : "",
      category: "",
      aircraft: get("aircraft") != null ? String(get("aircraft")).trim() : "",
      status,
      expiryDate,
      storeType: detectStoreType(storeHint) || defaultStoreType,
      remarks: get("remarks") != null ? String(get("remarks")).trim() : "",
    });
  }
  return { items };
}

function ImportPage({ data, setData }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("file");
  const [pasteText, setPasteText] = useState("");
  const [defaultStore, setDefaultStore] = useState("Main");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(null); // { items, error }
  const [done, setDone] = useState(null);

  const handleFile = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (evt) => {
      try {
        const wb = XLSX.read(evt.target.result, { type: "array" });
        const ws = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
        const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws, { header: 1, raw: false, defval: "" });
        setPreview(parseRowsToItems(rows, defaultStore));
      } catch (err) {
        setPreview({ error: "Could not read that file. Make sure it's a valid .xlsx, .xls, or .csv." });
      }
    };
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  };

  const handlePasteParse = () => {
    const rows = pasteText.split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0).map((line) => line.split("\t"));
    setPreview(parseRowsToItems(rows, defaultStore));
  };

  const commitImport = () => {
    if (!preview?.items?.length) return;
    setData({ ...data, items: [...data.items, ...preview.items] });
    setDone(preview.items.length);
    setPreview(null);
    setPasteText("");
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Permanent · Once Imported It Stays" title="Bulk Import" />

      <Card style={{ padding: 20, maxWidth: 720 }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <Btn variant={mode === "file" ? "primary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => { setMode("file"); setPreview(null); }}><UploadCloud size={14} /> Upload Excel / CSV</Btn>
          <Btn variant={mode === "paste" ? "primary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => { setMode("paste"); setPreview(null); }}><ClipboardPaste size={14} /> Paste from Excel</Btn>
        </div>

        <Field label="Default Store (used when the sheet doesn't say Main / Bonded / POL / Fridge)">
          <Select options={STORE_TYPES} value={defaultStore} onChange={(e) => setDefaultStore(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>

        {mode === "file" ? (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
            <input type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv" onChange={handleFile} style={{ fontSize: 13 }} />
            <div style={{ fontSize: 11.5, color: STEEL, marginTop: 8 }}>
              Works with stock sheets that have columns like Description, Part No, Qty, Unit, Aircraft, Location, Serial No, Batch No, Condition, Expiry. Extra columns are ignored.
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
            <textarea
              value={pasteText}
              onChange={(e) => setPasteText(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Select your rows in Excel (including the header row), copy, and paste here…"
              style={{ ...inputStyle, width: "100%", minHeight: 160, fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", fontSize: 12 }}
            />
            <div style={{ marginTop: 10 }}><Btn onClick={handlePasteParse} disabled={!pasteText.trim()}>Parse Pasted Data</Btn></div>
          </div>
        )}

        {preview?.error && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 16, color: RED, fontSize: 13, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
            <AlertTriangle size={15} /> {preview.error}
          </div>
        )}

        {preview?.items && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 18 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 8 }}>
              <Placard>{preview.items.length} item{preview.items.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""} detected — preview below</Placard>
              <Btn onClick={commitImport}><CheckCircle2 size={14} /> Import All {preview.items.length}</Btn>
            </div>
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 320, overflowY: "auto", border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, borderRadius: 4 }}>
              <Table columns={["Description", "Part No", "Qty", "Store", "Location"]}
                rows={preview.items.slice(0, 30).map((it) => [it.description, it.partNumber || "—", `${it.qty} ${it.unit}`, it.storeType, it.location || "—"])} />
            </div>
            {preview.items.length > 30 && <div style={{ fontSize: 11.5, color: STEEL, marginTop: 6 }}>+ {preview.items.length - 30} more not shown in preview.</div>}
          </div>
        )}

        {done !== null && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 16, padding: "10px 14px", background: "#EFF6F1", border: `1px solid ${GREEN}`, borderRadius: 4, color: GREEN, fontSize: 13, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 8 }}>
            <CheckCircle2 size={15} /> Imported {done} item{done !== 1 ? "s" : ""} into inventory — saved permanently.
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  REPORTS & EXPORT                                                        */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function ExportPage({ data }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const byId = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(data.items.map((i) => [i.id, i])), [data.items]);

  const buildWorkbook = () => {
    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

    const itemRow = (it) => ({
      Description: it.description, "Part No": it.partNumber, "Serial No": it.serialNumber, "Batch No": it.batchNumber,
      Qty: it.qty, Unit: it.unit, Location: it.location, Category: it.category, Aircraft: it.aircraft,
      Status: it.status, "Expiry Date": it.expiryDate, "Store Type": it.storeType, Remarks: it.remarks,
    });

    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.items.map(itemRow)), "Available Stock (All)");
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "Main").map(itemRow)), "Main Inventory");
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "Bonded").map(itemRow)), "Bonded Store");
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "POL Cupboard" || i.storeType === "Fridge").map(itemRow)), "POL & Fridge");

    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.issues.map((r) => ({
      Date: r.date?.slice(0, 10), Item: byId[r.itemId]?.description || "", "Part No": byId[r.itemId]?.partNumber || "",
      Qty: r.qty, Unit: r.unit, "Issued To": r.issuedTo, Aircraft: r.aircraft, Location: r.location, Purpose: r.purpose,
    }))), "Issue Register");

    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.dispatches.map((r) => ({
      "Dispatch No": r.dispatchNo, Date: r.date?.slice(0, 10), Item: byId[r.itemId]?.description || "", "Part No": byId[r.itemId]?.partNumber || "",
      Qty: r.qty, Unit: r.unit, Destination: r.destination, "Dispatch To": r.dispatchTo, "Dispatch By": r.dispatchBy, Aircraft: r.aircraft,
    }))), "Dispatch Register");

    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.transfers.map((r) => ({
      Date: r.date?.slice(0, 10), Item: byId[r.itemId]?.description || "", Qty: r.qty, From: r.from, To: r.to,
    }))), "Transfer History");

    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.discards.map((r) => ({
      Date: r.date?.slice(0, 10), Item: byId[r.itemId]?.description || "", Qty: r.qty, Reason: r.reason,
    }))), "Discard Register");

    const expired = data.items.filter((i) => i.expiryDate && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) < 0);
    const expiring = data.items.filter((i) => i.expiryDate && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) >= 0 && daysUntil(i.expiryDate) <= 10);
    const low = data.items.filter((i) => Number(i.qty) <= 2);
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet([...expired, ...expiring, ...low].map((it) => ({ ...itemRow(it), Flag: expired.includes(it) ? "EXPIRED" : expiring.includes(it) ? "EXPIRING SOON" : "LOW QTY" }))), "Low Stock & Expiry");

    return wb;
  };

  const download = () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const wb = buildWorkbook();
      XLSX.writeFile(wb, `Aviation-Store-Report-${todayISO()}.xlsx`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const sections = [
    ["Main Inventory", data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "Main").length],
    ["Bonded Store", data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "Bonded").length],
    ["POL & Fridge", data.items.filter((i) => i.storeType === "POL Cupboard" || i.storeType === "Fridge").length],
    ["Issue Register", data.issues.length],
    ["Dispatch Register", data.dispatches.length],
    ["Transfer History", data.transfers.length],
    ["Discard Register", data.discards.length],
  ];

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="One Workbook, Every Register" title="Reports & Export"
        action={<Btn onClick={download} disabled={busy}><FileSpreadsheet size={14} /> Download Full Excel Report</Btn>} />
      <Card style={{ padding: 4 }}>
        <Table columns={["Sheet", "Rows"]} rows={sections.map(([name, count]) => [name, count])} />
      </Card>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: STEEL, marginTop: 12 }}>
        The workbook includes available stock split by store, plus full issue, dispatch, transfer, and discard registers — and a colour-flagged low stock & expiry sheet.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  MASTERS                                                                 */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function MasterList({ label, list, onAdd, onRemove }) {
  const [val, setVal] = useState("");
  return (
    <Card style={{ padding: 16 }}>
      <Placard style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 10 }}>{label}</Placard>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, marginBottom: 12 }}>
        <Input value={val} onChange={(e) => setVal(e.target.value)} placeholder={`Add ${label.toLowerCase()}…`} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <Btn onClick={() => { if (val.trim()) { onAdd(val.trim()); setVal(""); } }}><Plus size={14} /></Btn>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 6 }}>
        {list.map((v) => (
          <span key={v} style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, padding: "5px 8px", borderRadius: 3, border: `1px solid ${LINE}`, fontSize: 12 }}>
            {v}
            <X size={12} style={{ cursor: "pointer", color: STEEL }} onClick={() => onRemove(v)} />
          </span>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <span style={{ color: STEEL, fontSize: 12 }}>None yet.</span>}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function MastersPage({ data, setData, flags, setPage, thresholds }) {
  const update = (key, next) => setData({ ...data, masters: { ...data.masters, [key]: next } });
  const add = (key) => (v) => { if (!data.masters[key].includes(v)) update(key, [...data.masters[key], v]); };
  const remove = (key) => (v) => update(key, data.masters[key].filter((x) => x !== v));

  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Configuration" title="Settings" />

      {flags && (
        <Card style={{ padding: 18, marginBottom: 20, borderColor: (flags.expired || flags.expiring || flags.low) ? AMBER : LINE }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 10 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
              <AlertTriangle size={16} color={AMBER} />
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13.5 }}>Stock Alerts</div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: STEEL, marginTop: 2 }}>
                  {flags.expired} expired · {flags.expiring} expiring within {thresholds?.expiryWarnDays ?? 10} days · {flags.low} at or below {thresholds?.lowStockThreshold ?? 2} qty
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Btn variant="ghost" onClick={() => setPage("lowstock")}>View Full Report <ChevronRight size={14} /></Btn>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Placard style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 10 }}>Configurable Lists</Placard>
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 16 }}>
        <MasterList label="Aircraft" list={data.masters.aircraft} onAdd={add("aircraft")} onRemove={remove("aircraft")} />
        <MasterList label="Locations" list={data.masters.locations} onAdd={add("locations")} onRemove={remove("locations")} />
        <MasterList label="Persons" list={data.masters.persons} onAdd={(v) => add("persons")(v.toUpperCase())} onRemove={remove("persons")} />
        <MasterList label="Categories" list={data.masters.categories} onAdd={add("categories")} onRemove={remove("categories")} />
        <MasterList label="Units" list={data.masters.units} onAdd={add("units")} onRemove={remove("units")} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
